Let me write these carefully.

First, context_atoms:
```
Fixpoint context_atoms (C : program_context) : list atom :=
  match C with
  | C_Hole => []
  | C_Abs T C' κ => context_atoms C'
  | C_AppLeft C' t2 r => context_atoms C' ++ fv t2
  | C_AppRight t1 C' r => fv t1 ++ context_atoms C'
  | C_PairLeft C' t2 κ => context_atoms C' ++ fv t2
  | C_PairRight t1 C' κ => fv t1 ++ context_atoms C'
  | C_Fst C' r => context_atoms C'
  | C_Snd C' r => context_atoms C'
  | C_Inl C' κ => context_atoms C'
  | C_Inr C' κ => context_atoms C'
  | C_CaseScrutinee C' b1 b2 r => context_atoms C' ++ fv b1 ++ fv b2
  | C_CaseLeft t0 C' b2 r => fv t0 ++ context_atoms C' ++ fv b2
  | C_CaseRight t0 b1 C' r => fv t0 ++ fv b1 ++ context_atoms C'
  | C_Protect l C' => context_atoms C'
  | C_IfCondition C' y n r => context_atoms C' ++ fv y ++ fv n
  | C_IfThen c C' n r => fv c ++ context_atoms C' ++ fv n
  | C_IfElse c y C' r => fv c ++ fv y ++ context_atoms C'
  end.
```
Then a lemma: for each sibling term t in C, x ∉ context_atoms C → x ∉ fv t. When proving plug_subst_env by induction on C, the sibling terms' fv appear in context_atoms of the enclosing context; the induction hypothesis for C' gets ~ In x (context_atoms C'), derived from ~ In x (context_atoms (C_... C' ...)) via list inclusion. Let me prove a general helper: `context_atoms_incl : forall C' t r ..., In x (context_atoms C') -> In x (context_atoms (C_AppLeft C' t r))` etc. Since context_atoms is built with ++, we need the In_app lemmas. Rather than proving a separate inclusion lemma per constructor, in plug_subst_env's induction step we get Hx : ~ In x (context_atoms (C_AppLeft C' t2 r)) = ~ In x (context_atoms C' ++ fv t2); we can derive ~ In x (context_atoms C') and ~ In x (fv t2) via In_app_iff. Let me write:
```
Lemma not_in_app_l : forall x l1 l2, ~ In x (l1 ++ l2) -> ~ In x l1.
Proof. intros x l1 l2 H Hin. apply H. apply In_app_l. exact Hin. Qed.
Lemma not_in_app_r : forall x l1 l2, ~ In x (l1 ++ l2) -> ~ In x l2.
Proof. intros x l1 l2 H Hin. apply H. apply In_app_r. exact Hin. Qed.
```
Then in each constructor case of plug_subst_env, use these.

plug_subst_env:
```
Lemma plug_subst_env : forall C x t,
    ~ In x (context_atoms C) ->
    subst_env (extend_env x t (fun y => tm_fvar y)) (plug C (tm_fvar x)) = plug C t.
Proof.
  induction C; simpl; intros x t Hx; auto.
```
Hmm, the cases: C_Hole: plug = fvar x; subst_env (extend_env ...) (fvar x) = extend_env x t id x = t = plug C_Hole t ✓; need unfold extend_env; rewrite Nat.eqb_refl.
C_Abs T C' κ: plug = tm_abs T (plug C' (fvar x)) κ; subst_env = tm_abs T (subst_env σ (plug C' ...)) κ; IH applies with Hx : ~ In x (context_atoms (C_Abs T C' κ)) = ~ In x (context_atoms C') ✓.
C_AppLeft C' t2 r: plug = tm_app (plug C' (fvar x)) t2 r; subst_env = tm_app (subst_env σ (plug C' ...)) (subst_env σ t2) r; IH with Hx1 = not_in_app_l; and subst_env σ t2 = t2 via subst_env_extend_id with not_in_app_r ✓.

Let me write:
```
Lemma plug_subst_env : forall C x t,
    ~ In x (context_atoms C) ->
    subst_env (extend_env x t (fun y => tm_fvar y)) (plug C (tm_fvar x)) = plug C t.
Proof.
  induction C; simpl; intros x t Hx; try (f_equal; auto; fail).
```
Hmm, `try (f_equal; auto; fail)` may not work well. Let me write all 17 cases explicitly:

Constructors:
1. C_Hole.
2. C_Abs T C' κ.
3. C_AppLeft C' t2 r.
4. C_AppRight t1 C' r.

Note the induction hypotheses: for C with one subcontext, IHC : forall x t, ~ In x (context_atoms C) -> ...; for two subcontexts (none here—all contexts have at most one subcontext) so simple.

Let me write the proofs:
- C_Hole: goal subst_env σ (tm_fvar x) = t, i.e., extend_env x t id x = t. `unfold extend_env. rewrite Nat.eqb_refl. reflexivity.`
- C_Abs: goal tm_abs T (subst_env σ (plug C (fvar x))) κ = tm_abs T (plug C t) κ → f_equal; apply IHC; exact Hx (maybe need unfold context_atoms in Hx; simpl in Hx does it).
- Binary contexts with a sibling s: f_equal; [apply IHC; apply not_in_app_l; exact Hx | apply subst_env_extend_id; apply not_in_app_r; exact Hx]. But the order of conjuncts in the goal after f_equal: for tm_app (plug ...) s r, f_equal generates 2 goals: the plug part and the sibling part (r same) ✓.
  For Right contexts where the sibling comes first: tm_app s (plug ...) r: f_equal gives sibling then plug; the order of not_in_app changes: context_atoms (C_AppRight t1 C' r) = fv t1 ++ context_atoms C'; so the plug is on the right: apply IHC with not_in_app_r; sibling subst via not_in_app_l.
- Unary contexts: f_equal; apply IHC; exact Hx.
- Case contexts: 
  C_CaseScrutinee C' b1 b2 r: context_atoms = context_atoms C' ++ fv b1 ++ fv b2 (right-assoc: context_atoms C' ++ (fv b1 ++ fv b2)); plug = tm_case (plug C' ..) b1 b2 r; f_equal gives 3 goals: plug, b1, b2. plug: IHC with not_in_app_l Hx; b1: not_in_app_r Hx then not_in_app_l; b2: not_in_app_r Hx then not_in_app_r.
  C_CaseLeft t0 C' b2 r: context_atoms = fv t0 ++ context_atoms C' ++ fv b2 right-assoc: fv t0 ++ (context_atoms C' ++ fv b2). plug = case t0 (plug C'..) b2 r; f_equal goals: t0, plug, b2. t0: not_in_app_l Hx; plug: not_in_app_r Hx then not_in_app_l; b2: not_in_app_r Hx then not_in_app_r.
  C_CaseRight t0 b1 C' r: fv t0 ++ (fv b1 ++ context_atoms C'); goals t0, b1, plug: not_in_app_l; not_in_app_r then not_in_app_l; not_in_app_r then not_in_app_r.
- If contexts analogous with 3 term parts.

The `f_equal` order: for tm_case, f_equal decomposes into 3 (or more?) goals. Since the term has 3 subterms and a label, f_equal generates 3 subgoals (the label is the same). subst_env_extend_id:
```
Lemma subst_env_extend_id : forall x t s,
    ~ In x (fv s) -> subst_env (extend_env x t (fun y => tm_fvar y)) s = s.
Proof.
  intros x t s Hx. rewrite <- subst_env_fvar_id.
  apply subst_env_ext. intros y Hy.
  unfold extend_env. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
  - apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y. exfalso. apply Hx. exact Hy.
  - reflexivity.
Qed.
```
Note subst_env_fvar_id : subst_env (fun x => tm_fvar x) t = t. `rewrite <- subst_env_fvar_id` rewrites the goal `subst_env σ s = s` into `subst_env σ s = subst_env id s` ✓ (rewriting the RHS s). Then subst_env_ext ✓.

Then final theorem needs `plug_subst_env` at σ = extend_env x t id ✓.

    has_type Γ t T ->

    ...

For all_visible and VR_erase, let me write them.

```
Fixpoint all_visible (l : label) (T : ty) : Prop :=
  flows_to (security_of T).(indirect_reader) l /\
  match T with
  | Ty_Unit _ => True
  | Ty_Sum T1 T2 _ | Ty_Prod T1 T2 _ | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 _ =>
      all_visible l T1 /\ all_visible l T2
  end.

Lemma transparent_all_visible : forall T κ l,
    transparent_at κ T -> flows_to κ.(indirect_reader) l -> all_visible l T.
Proof.
  induction T; intros κ l Ht Hflow; simpl in *;
  try (split; [| trivial]).
  - (* unit *) destruct Ht as [Hle _]. eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hle | exact Hflow].
```
Hmm, for the unit case, after `simpl in *`, Ht : security_le (security_of (Ty_Unit s)) κ /\ True. Let me destruct. Let me write each case explicitly:
```
  - (* unit *) destruct Ht as [Hle _]. simpl in Hle. split; [| trivial]. eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hle | exact Hflow].
  - (* sum *) destruct Ht as [Hle [Ht1 Ht2]]. split; [| split].
    + eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hle | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT1; [exact Ht1 | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT2; [exact Ht2 | exact Hflow].
  ...
```
Careful: IHT1 : forall κ l, transparent_at κ T1 -> flows_to κ.ind l -> all_visible l T1 ✓.
But `simpl in Hle` for the sum: Hle : security_le (security_of (Ty_Sum T1 T2 s)) κ which is security_le s κ; `simpl in Hle` might unfold security_le to the conjunction; we need only the indirect component: `unfold security_le in Hle; apply Hle`? eapply flows_to_trans with (l2 := κ.ind): need flows_to s.ind κ.ind which is the second component of security_le. So destruct Hle as [_ Hle_i] after unfolding.

Let me write:
```
  - (* sum *)
    destruct Ht as [Hle [Ht1 Ht2]]. unfold security_le in Hle. destruct Hle as [_ Hle_i].
    split; [| split].
    + eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hle_i | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT1; [exact Ht1 | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT2; [exact Ht2 | exact Hflow].
```

VR_erase:
```
Lemma VR_erase : forall l T v1 v2,
    ground T -> all_visible l T -> VR l T v1 v2 ->
    erase_security v1 = erase_security v2.
Proof.
  induction T as [κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ];
  intros v1 v2 Hg Hv HVR; simpl in Hg, Hv.
  - (* unit *) destruct Hv as [Hflow _].
    apply flowb_true in Hflow.
    cbn [VR VRp] in HVR. repeat rewrite join_low_r in HVR. rewrite Hflow in HVR.
    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; reflexivity.
  - (* sum *)
    destruct Hv as [Hflow [Hv1 Hv2]]; destruct Hg as [Hg1 Hg2].
    apply flowb_true in Hflow.
    cbn [VR VRp] in HVR. repeat rewrite join_low_r in HVR. rewrite Hflow in HVR.
    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; simpl.
    + f_equal. apply IH1; [exact Hg1 | exact Hv1 | exact HVR].
    + f_equal. apply IH2; [exact Hg2 | exact Hv2 | exact HVR].
  - (* prod *)
    destruct Hv as [Hflow [Hv1 Hv2]]; destruct Hg as [Hg1 Hg2].
    apply flowb_true in Hflow.
    cbn [VR VRp] in HVR. repeat rewrite join_low_r in HVR. rewrite Hflow in HVR.
    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; simpl.
    destruct HVR as [Hr1 Hr2]. f_equal.
    + apply IH1; [exact Hg1 | exact Hv1 | exact Hr1].
    + apply IH2; [exact Hg2 | exact Hv2 | exact Hr2].
  - (* arrow *) destruct Hg.
Qed.
```
Wait, for the unit case, `HVR` after cbn and rewriting Hflow: HVR : match v1,v2 with tm_unit _,tm_unit _ => True | _,_ => False end. destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; ... ; reflexivity. In the surviving case v1 = tm_unit k1, v2 = tm_unit k2; the goal erase (unit k1) = erase (unit k2) → both tm_unit public → reflexivity ✓.

For sum: after destructing, in the inl/inl case HVR : VR l T1 Low v1 v2 = VR l T1 v1 v2; goal: erase (inl v1 κ1) = erase (inl v2 κ2) → simpl gives tm_inl (erase v1) public = tm_inl (erase v2) public; f_equal; apply IH1 ✓.

Hmm, `simpl` before f_equal: the goal `erase_security (tm_inl v1 κ1) = erase_security (tm_inl v2 κ2)`; `simpl` reduces erase_security to tm_inl (erase v1) public = tm_inl (erase v2) public ✓.

For prod: similar with pair.

But careful: in the destruct sequence, the constructors of tm include tm_inl etc.; after `destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction`, the inl case remains with HVR : VR l T1 Low v1 v2; but also the inr case remains (for sum): two surviving cases for each v1 shape? Let's see: v1 = tm_inl u k: the match: first pattern requires v1 inl → matches if v2 inl; second requires v1 inr → fails; else False. So after destruct v1 = inl, destruct v2: inl → HVR payload; other → False. So after both destructs, surviving: (inl,inl) and (inr,inr) → 2 goals ✓ bullets in order? The destruct v1 gives 13 goals, contradiction closes 11; the inl and inr goals remain; destruct v2 on each gives 26; contradiction leaves 2: (inl,inl) first, then (inr,inr) ✓.

For prod: 1 surviving goal ✓.

```
Theorem noninterference : ...
Proof.
  intros C t1 t2 T_secret T_result Ht1 Ht2 Hctx Hgnd Htr Hnflow.
  destruct Hctx as [L Hctx].
  destruct (exists_fresh_two (L ++ context_atoms C) (fv t1 ++ fv t2)) as (x & Hx1 & Hx2).
  assert (HxL : ~ In x L) by (intro H; apply Hx1; apply In_app_l; exact H).
  assert (HxC : ~ In x (context_atoms C)) by (intro H; apply Hx1; apply In_app_r; exact H).
  assert (Hxt1 : ~ In x (fv t1)) by (intro H; apply Hx2; apply In_app_l; exact H).
  assert (Hxt2 : ~ In x (fv t2)) by (intro H; apply Hx2; apply In_app_r; exact H).
  set (σ1 := extend_env x t1 (fun y => tm_fvar y)).
  set (σ2 := extend_env x t2 (fun y => tm_fvar y)).
  assert (Hplug1 : subst_env σ1 (plug C (tm_fvar x)) = plug C t1).
  { subst σ1. apply plug_subst_env. exact HxC. }
  assert (Hplug2 : subst_env σ2 (plug C (tm_fvar x)) = plug C t2).
  { subst σ2. apply plug_subst_env. exact HxC. }
  assert (Henv : EnvRel (security_of T_result).(indirect_reader) (update empty x T_secret) σ1 σ2).
  { split; [| split].
    - intro y. subst σ1. unfold extend_env. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [apply has_type_lc; exact Ht1 | trivial].
    - intro y. subst σ2. unfold extend_env. destruct (Nat.eqb y x); [apply has_type_lc; exact Ht2 | trivial].
    - intros y T Hy. subst σ1; subst σ2. unfold extend_env. destruct (Nat.eqb y x) eqn:E.
      + apply Nat.eqb_eq in E. subst y. rewrite lookup_update_same in Hy. injection Hy as Hy. subst T.
        apply TR_hidden. apply flowb_false. exact Hnflow.
      + apply Nat.eqb_neq in E. rewrite lookup_update_other in Hy; [| exact E].
        (* lookup y empty = Some T is impossible *)
        simpl in Hy. discriminate Hy.
  }
```
Hmm, the lookup case: update empty x T_secret = (x,T_secret)::[]; lookup y [(x,T_secret)]: if y =? x then Some T_secret else lookup y [] = None. In the y≠x branch, lookup y empty = None; but Hy : lookup y empty = Some T → contradiction. `simpl in Hy; discriminate Hy` — lookup_context y [] = None, so Hy : None = Some T; discriminate ✓.

Wait, but `update empty x T_secret` where empty = [] — update is (x,T)::[] ✓.

  pose proof (fundamental (security_of T_result).(indirect_reader) (update empty x T_secret) (plug C (tm_fvar x)) T_result (Hctx x HxL) σ1 σ2 Henv) as HTR.
  rewrite Hplug1 in HTR. rewrite Hplug2 in HTR.

```

```
Lemma plug_subst_env : forall C x u,
    ~ In x (context_atoms C) ->
    subst_env (extend_env x u (fun y => tm_fvar y)) (plug C (tm_fvar x)) = plug C u.
Proof.
  induction C; simpl; intros x u Hx.
  - (* C_Hole *) rewrite (extend_env_same x u (fun y => tm_fvar y)). reflexivity.
  - (* C_Abs *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_AppLeft *) f_equal. apply IHC. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_AppRight *) f_equal. apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_PairLeft *) f_equal. apply IHC. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_PairRight *) f_equal. apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_Fst *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_Snd *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_Inl *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_Inr *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_CaseScrutinee *) f_equal.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_CaseLeft *) f_equal.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_CaseRight *) f_equal.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_Protect *) f_equal. apply IHC. exact Hx.
  - (* C_IfCondition *) f_equal.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_IfThen *) f_equal.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
  - (* C_IfElse *) f_equal.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply subst_env_extend_id. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_l. exact Hx.
    + apply IHC. apply not_in_app_r. apply not_in_app_r. exact Hx.
Qed.
```

Let me write them.

```
Fixpoint all_visible (l : label) (T : ty) : Prop :=
  flows_to (security_of T).(indirect_reader) l /\
  match T with
  | Ty_Unit _ => True
  | Ty_Sum T1 T2 _ | Ty_Prod T1 T2 _ | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 _ =>
      all_visible l T1 /\ all_visible l T2
  end.

Lemma transparent_all_visible : forall T κ l,
    transparent_at κ T -> flows_to κ.(indirect_reader) l -> all_visible l T.
Proof.
  induction T; intros κ l Ht Hflow; simpl in Ht.
  - (* unit *) destruct Ht as [Hle _]. unfold security_le in Hle. destruct Hle as [_ Hi].
    split; [| trivial]. eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hi | exact Hflow].
  - (* sum *) destruct Ht as [Hle [Ht1 Ht2]]. unfold security_le in Hle. destruct Hle as [_ Hi].
    split; [| split].
    + eapply flows_to_trans; [exact Hi | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT1; [exact Ht1 | exact Hflow].
    + apply IHT2; [exact Ht2 | exact Hflow].
  - (* prod *) similar
  - (* arrow *) similar
Qed.
```
Hmm, note the constructor order in ty: Ty_Unit, Ty_Sum, Ty_Prod, Ty_Arrow → induction gives 4 cases ✓.

For sums: transparent_at κ (Ty_Sum T1 T2 κ0) = security_le (security_of T) κ /\ (transparent_at κ T1 /\ transparent_at κ T2) ✓. After `simpl in Ht`, Ht might be exactly that. destruct as [Hle [Ht1 Ht2]] ✓.

Lemma VR_erase : forall l T v1 v2,
    ground T -> all_visible l T -> VR l T v1 v2 ->
    erase_security v1 = erase_security v2.
Proof.
  induction T as [κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ | T1 IH1 T2 IH2 κ];
  intros v1 v2 Hg Hv HVR; simpl in Hg, Hv.
  - (* unit *) destruct Hv as [Hflow _].
    apply flowb_true in Hflow.
    cbn [VR VRp] in HVR. repeat rewrite join_low_r in HVR. rewrite Hflow in HVR.
    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction;
    reflexivity.
```
Hmm: `cbn [VR VRp] in HVR` reduces VRp to the if; then repeat rewrite join_low_r; then rewrite Hflow where Hflow : flowb κ.ind l = true. But rewriting a boolean equality into an if may hit the same setoid issue! Earlier we saw `rewrite E` into an if condition fails with setoid errors in some contexts. In VR_*_intro/inv I used `rewrite E in H` where E : flowb ... = true and it worked (because the if's scrutinee is exactly that boolean term; rewriting bool equalities inside if is allowed? It worked in VR_unit_inv, so fine). Here `cbn [VR VRp]` replaces VRp with the if-expression whose condition is flowb (join κ.ind Low) l; after repeat rewrite join_low_r, the condition is flowb κ.ind l; rewrite Hflow should work as in VR_unit_inv ✓.

Alternatively, use `destruct (flowb κ.ind l) eqn:E` and in the false branch use Hflow to derive contradiction. Let me use the rewrite approach as in the intro lemmas (which compiled).

  - (* sum *)
    destruct Hg as [Hg1 Hg2]. destruct Hv as [Hflow [Hv1 Hv2]].
    apply flowb_true in Hflow.
    cbn [VR VRp] in HVR. repeat rewrite join_low_r in HVR. rewrite Hflow in HVR.
    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; simpl.
    + f_equal. apply IH1; [exact Hg1 | exact Hv1 | exact HVR].

```

    destruct v1; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; destruct v2; cbn in HVR; try contradiction; simpl.
    destruct HVR as [Hr1 Hr2]. f_equal.
    + apply IH1; [exact Hg1 | exact Hv1 | exact Hr1].
    + apply IH2; [exact Hg2 | exact Hv2 | exact Hr2].

Now the final theorem.

```
Theorem noninterference : ... 
Proof.
  intros C t1 t2 T_secret T_result Ht1 Ht2 Hctx Hgnd Htr Hnflow.
  destruct Hctx as [L Hctx].
  destruct (exists_fresh (L ++ context_atoms C)) as (x & Hx).
  assert (HxL : ~ In x L) by (intro H; apply Hx; apply In_app_l; exact H).
  assert (HxC : ~ In x (context_atoms C)) by (intro H; apply Hx; apply In_app_r; exact H).
  set (l := (security_of T_result).(indirect_reader)).
